# 077 profile panel report

Machine-readable transcript: [report.json](report.json). Generated by `npm run profile:panel`; it
contains no wall-clock value, random value or browser-only state.

Version: `077-profile-panel-v1`. The report drives the real `createProfilePanel` adapter over the
real catalog defaults and a real 109-style `DrivingProfile`.

## Contract

States: OBSERVED - the published record has a value for the key; a real 0 is displayed as 0; UNOBSERVED - no value in the record; value is null and displayed as Neobservat; RESERVED - an R/extension key; always null, not learnable in V1, even with a numeric record value.

Rules: illustrative frames use the real catalog defaults and never claim a publication; reserved keys never appear as learned delta changes; a delta must use the exact parent of the rendered version; evidence is shown only from the record; a missing count is 0; the panel has no controls and never mutates, publishes or activates a profile.

Banner: `Date ilustrative — serviciul de profiluri nu a publicat încă o versiune`.

Limits: 10 Hz write ceiling, 80 catalog keys, 80 delta changes and 3 decimals.
